theorem law_of_cosines_triangle_inequality (x y z α β γ : ℝ) (hx : 0 < x) (hy : 0 < y) (hz : 0 < z) (hα₀ : 0 ≤ α) (hαπ : α < Real.pi) (hβ₀ : 0 ≤ β) (hβπ : β < Real.pi) (hγ₀ : 0 ≤ γ) (hγπ : γ < Real.pi) (hαβγ : γ < α + β) (hβγα : α < β + γ) (hγαβ : β < γ + α) : Real.sqrt (x ^ 2 + y ^ 2 - 2 * x * y * Real.cos α) + Real.sqrt (y ^ 2 + z ^ 2 - 2 * y * z * Real.cos β) ≥ Real.sqrt (z ^ 2 + x ^ 2 - 2 * z * x * Real.cos γ) := by sorry

theorem sum_quotient_minimum (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) (x : ℝ) (hx : (n : ℝ) ≤ x) : (n : ℝ) + 1 ≤ x + (n : ℝ) / x ∧ (x + (n : ℝ) / x = (n : ℝ) + 1 ↔ x = (n : ℝ)) := by sorry
theorem prime_pairwise_products_solution (p q r n : ℕ) (hp : Nat.Prime p) (hq : Nat.Prime q) (hr : Nat.Prime r) (h : p * q + p * r + q * r = 12 * n + 1) : n = 5 ∧ ((p = 2 ∧ q = 3 ∧ r = 11) ∨ (p = 2 ∧ q = 11 ∧ r = 3) ∨ (p = 3 ∧ q = 2 ∧ r = 11) ∨ (p = 3 ∧ q = 11 ∧ r = 2) ∨ (p = 11 ∧ q = 2 ∧ r = 3) ∨ (p = 11 ∧ q = 3 ∧ r = 2)) := by sorry
theorem quotient_minimum : IsLeast {q : ℝ | ∃ x : ℝ, 0 < x ∧ x < Real.pi ∧ q = (9 * (x * Real.sin x) ^ 2 + 4) / (x * Real.sin x)} 12 := by sorry
theorem trigonometric_inequality (a b c : ℝ) (ha_pos : 0 < a) (hb_pos : 0 < b) (hc_pos : 0 < c) (ha_lt : a < Real.pi / 2) (hb_lt : b < Real.pi / 2) (hc_lt : c < Real.pi / 2) : Real.pi / 2 + 2 * Real.sin a * Real.cos b + 2 * Real.sin b * Real.cos c > Real.sin (2 * a) + Real.sin (2 * b) + Real.sin (2 * c) := by sorry
theorem ten_angles_sine_cosine_inequality (θ : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hθ : ∀ i, 0 ≤ θ i ∧ θ i ≤ Real.pi / 2) (hsum : ∑ i, (Real.sin (θ i)) ^ 2 = 1) : 3 * (∑ i, Real.sin (θ i)) ≤ ∑ i, Real.cos (θ i) := by sorry
theorem product_nonpositive_iff (x : ℝ) : (-4 : ℝ) * (x - 5) * (x + 4) * (x - 1) ≤ 0 ↔ ((-4 ≤ x ∧ x ≤ 1) ∨ 5 ≤ x) := by sorry
theorem sum_triple_products_le (a b c d : ℝ) (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hb : 0 ≤ b) (hc : 0 ≤ c) (hd : 0 ≤ d) (hsum : a + b + c + d = 1) : a * b * c + a * b * d + a * c * d + b * c * d ≤ (1 : ℝ) / 27 + (176 : ℝ) / 27 * (a * b * c * d) := by sorry
theorem integer_pair_count : Set.ncard {p : ℤ × ℤ | p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2 < 16 ∧ p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2 < 8 * p.1 ∧ p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2 < 8 * p.2} = 6 := by sorry
theorem unique_positive_quadruple (a b c d : ℕ) (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (hc : 0 < c) (hd : 0 < d) : 20 ^ a + 14 ^ (2 * b) = (a + 2 * b + c) ^ (c * d) ↔ a = 1 ∧ b = 1 ∧ c = 3 ∧ d = 1 := by sorry
theorem exists_initial_segment_sum_ge_polynomial (a : List ℝ) (ha : a ≠ []) : ∃ n : ℕ, 0 < n ∧ n ≤ a.length ∧ ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc (-1 : ℝ) 1, (a.enum.map (fun p => p.2 * x ^ p.1)).sum ≤ (a.take n).sum := by sorry
theorem largest_prime_divisor_below_one_hundred : Nat.Prime 61 ∧ 61 < 100 ∧ 61 ∣ Nat.choose 200 100 ∧ ∀ p : ℕ, Nat.Prime p → p < 100 → p ∣ Nat.choose 200 100 → p ≤ 61 := by sorry
theorem minimum_sqrt_expression : (∀ x : ℝ, -1 ≤ x → x ≤ 1 → Real.sqrt 109 ≤ Real.sqrt (58 - 42 * x) + Real.sqrt (149 - 140 * Real.sqrt (1 - x ^ 2))) ∧ (∃ x : ℝ, -1 ≤ x ∧ x ≤ 1 ∧ Real.sqrt (58 - 42 * x) + Real.sqrt (149 - 140 * Real.sqrt (1 - x ^ 2)) = Real.sqrt 109) := by sorry
